AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Diagnosis\n\nThe app is Aegis Authenticator. The first-time-setup password screen is `CustomAuthenticatedSlide` (layout `fragment_authenticated_slide.xml`), whose AppIntro gate is `ISlidePolicy.isPolicyRespected()`.\n\nThe "show password" checkbox had been implemented as an **escape hatch from confirmation** rather than a pure visibility toggle:\n\n1. `onCheckedChanged` → when checked, it *disabled* the confirm field (`_textPasswordConfirm.setEnabled(false)`), so the user couldn\'t confirm even if they wanted to.\n2. `isPolicyRespected()` → `areEditTextsEqual(...) || _checkPasswordVisibility.isChecked()` — the `||` short-circuits, so a checked box made the policy pass with an empty, unconfirmed confirm field.\n3. `onUserIllegallyRequestedNextPage()` → `... && !_checkPasswordVisibility.isChecked()` suppressed the `password_equality_error` snackbar for the same reason.\n\nSo: type password → tick "show password" → Next ⇒ `isPolicyRespected()` returns `true` ⇒ AppIntro advances and the vault is created from the single unverified field. Exactly the reported symptom. Biometrics is incidental — it only gates the separate `_bioSlot == null` check.\n\nThe identical pattern existed in `Dialogs.showSetPasswordDialog()` (used when setting/changing the vault password from preferences), where the checkbox both disabled the confirm field and force-enabled the OK button. Same class of defect, so I fixed it too.\n\n## Fix\n\nThe checkbox is now purely presentational — it flips `TransformationMethod` on **both** fields and leaves the confirm field enabled. The confirmation requirement is unconditional:\n\n- `isPolicyRespected()` now returns just `areEditTextsEqual(_textPassword, _textPasswordConfirm)`. Note this doesn\'t lose the empty-password rejection: `EditTextHelper.areEditTextsEqual` already returns `false` when `password.length == 0`, which made the surrounding `length > 0` guard redundant.\n- The `CRYPT_TYPE_NONE` path and the biometric `_bioSlot == null` fallthrough are untouched, so unencrypted setup and the biometric prompt behave as before.\n- In `Dialogs`, the OK-button click guard and the `TextWatcher`\'s enable/disable both key off equality alone.\n\nBehaviour after the patch: with "show password" ticked, both fields render in clear text and Next is still blocked (with the `password_equality_error` snackbar) until the confirm field matches.\n\n## Verification\n\n`./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**.\n\nTwo notes on scope: the `switchToggleVisibility`/`_checkPasswordVisibility` fields are still referenced (they register the listener), so no unused-variable fallout. I also found a stray `bug-275_output.txt` in the repo root — it\'s a transcript artifact from a previous run of this task, not project source, and I left it alone. The unified diff is above and saved to `/tmp/fix-275.patch`; I could not produce it via `git diff` because the outer research repo gitignores `runs/`, so the project files are untracked.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 3315, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 58172, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 3315, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAWK5UAX4LqzbquehJKb', stop_reason=None, session_id='aa73a9ea-2a9a-487e-87ae-0d2efef18748', uuid='58f10826-9da4-4244-a131-74593d5534f7')
